summary = 'Approval Gap'\n        else:\n            summary = 'Missing Final Count, Approval Gap'\n\n        missing_final_flags.append(int(missing_final))\n missing_final_flags\n    formatted['Approval Gap'] = approval_gap_flags\n    formatted['Total Errors'] = total_errors\n    formatted['Error Summary'] = summaries\n\n    # Ensure exact 11 columns\n    formatted = formatted[\n        REQUIRED_FIRST7\n        + ['Missing Final Count', 'Approval Gap', 'Total Errors', 'Error Summary']\n    ]\n    if formatted.shape[1] != 11:\n        raise AssertionError(f'Formatted Data should have 11 columns, found {formatted.shape[1]}')\n\n    return formatted\n\n\ndef build_summary(formatted_df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    grouped = (\n        formatted_df\n        .groupby(['Facility', 'Session ID'], as_index=False)[['Missing Final Count', 'Approval Gap', 'Total Errors']]\n        .sum()\n        .rename(columns={'Missing Final Count': 'Missing Final Counts', 'Approval Gap': 'Approval Gaps'})\n    )\n\n    grouped = grouped[grouped['Total Errors'] > 0].copy()\n    grouped = grouped.sort_values(['Facility', 'Session ID'], ascending=[True, True], kind='mergesort')\n\n    # Grand totals across ALL rows (same as across error groups, but compute per instruction)\n    grand_missing = int(formatted_df['Missing Final Count'].sum())\n    grand_gaps = int(formatted_df['Approval Gap'].sum())\n    grand_total = int(formatted_df['Total Errors'].sum())\n\n    grand_row = pd.DataFrame([\n        {\n            'Facility': 'Grand Total',\n            'Session ID': '-',\n            'Missing Final Counts': grand_missing,\n            'Approval Gaps': grand_gaps,\n            'Total Errors': grand_total,\n        }\n    ])\n\n    summary = pd.concat([grouped, grand_row], ignore_index=True)\n\n    # Enforce exact header order\n    summary = summary[['Facility', 'Session ID', 'Missing Final Counts', 'Approval Gaps', 'Total Errors']]\n\n    return summary\n\n\ndef copy_overview_sheet(template_path: Path, dest_wb: Workbook, sheet_name: str = 'Overview') -> None:\n    tmpl = load_workbook(template_path)\n    if sheet_name not in tmpl.sheetnames:\n        raise KeyError(f'Template missing sheet: {sheet_name}')\n\n    src = tmpl[sheet_name]\n    dst = dest_wb.create_sheet(sheet_name)\n\n    # Copy sheet-level properties (best effort)\n    dst.sheet_format = copy(src.sheet_format)\n    dst.sheet_properties = copy(src.sheet_properties)\n    dst.page_margins = copy(src.page_margins)\n    dst.page_setup = copy(src.page_setup)\n    dst.print_options = copy(src.print_options)\n\n    # Copy row/column dimensions\n    for col_letter, dim in src.column_dimensions.items():\n row_idx, dim in src.row_dimensions.items():\n        dst.row_dimensions[row_idx].height = dim.height\n        dst.row_dimensions[row_idx].hidden = dim.hidden\n        dst.row_dimensions[row_idx].outlineLevel = dim.outlineLevel\n\n    # Copy merged cells\n    for merged_range in list(src.merged_cells.ranges):\n        dst.merge_cells(str(merged_range))\n\n    # Copy cell values + styles\n    for row in src.iter_rows(min_row=1, max_row=src.max_row, min_col=1, max_col=src.max_column):\n        for cell in row:\n            out = dst.cell(row=cell.row,          out.alignment = copy(cell.alignment)\n\n    # Copy freeze panes\n    dst.freeze_panes = src.freeze_panes\n\n\ndef write_df_to_sheet(ws, df: pd.DataFrame) -> None:\n    for r_idx, row in enumerate(dataframe_to_rows(df, index=False, header=True), start=1):\n        for c_idx, value in enumerate(row, start=1):\n            ws.cell(row=r_idx, column=c_idx, value=value)\n\n    # Basic formatting: bold header row\n    from openpyxl.styles import Font\n    bold = Font(bold=True)\n    for c in range(1, df.shape[1] + 1):\n        ws.cell(row=1, column=c).font = bold\n\n    # Autofit-ish: set width based on header/value lengths (bounded)\n    for i, col in enumerate(df.columns, start=1):\n        max_len = len(str(col))\n        for v in df[col].head(100).tolist():\n            if v is None:\n                continue\n            max_len = max(max_len, len(str(v)))\n        ws.column_dimensions[ws.cell(row=1, column=i).column_letter].width = min(max(10, max_len + 2),
